Catalogue · 30 Oct 2026

The source library

Deaths & burials

Civil deaths, burial registers, monumental inscriptions and notices. Reported ages and inferred birth years may be inaccurate.

2,478 collected reference items

By country & location

Ireland 222 referencesScotland 242 referencesEngland 122 referencesUnited States 703 referencesCanada 32 referencesAustralia 52 referencesNew Zealand 104 references

Use the collection search for a county, parish, townland or address. Newspaper country groups identify the place of publication; the subject may have lived elsewhere.

Collected references 2,478

Reference items in this project, not the total surviving archive. A reference can appear in several types or locations; several references can concern the same record. Countries are inferred from source descriptions and recorded places, so check the citation. Undated and ambiguous items remain available.
